- **sign-off-review**: One-pass sign-off review that decides whether a draft can go out. - **sign-off-the-house-way**: Before opening a PR, know which contributor sign-off THAT house requires and make sure it's present — the contracts differ by house and a missing one is an instant, automated "didn't read the rules" bounce. Eclipse projects (e.g. Jetty) gate every PR on an ECA plus a DCO `Signed-off-by` trailer on each commit; Apache projects (Solr/Lucene) use a CLA with the committer resolving on merge and need no per-commit trailer; other houses need neither. Check the house's contract first, not after a bot rejects you. Use at front-gate, before the first commit. - **signalman-estimate-stack-cost**: Pre-flight cost estimate for an OpenTofu stack before applying. Runs `tofu plan -json`, sums the create-actions against a static SKU × region cost table, returns the estimated monthly cents. Trigger when the user says "what would this stack cost", "estimate the bill before I apply", "is this scenario going to blow our budget", or any "dry-run cost" variant. Read-only; does NOT mutate cloud state. ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-estimate-stack-cost))
- **signalman-health-check**: Run signalman health probes against a target VM and summarize the result. Default behavior runs every probe declared in the active release's signalman.build.yaml. Triggers on "check health of &lt;target&gt;", "are the probes passing on &lt;target&gt;", "is &lt;target&gt; healthy". ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-health-check))
- **signalman-health-history**: Query past health-check results for a target's deployments, newest first. Returns per-deployment probe outcomes with timestamps. Trigger when the user says "show health history for &lt;target&gt;", "when did deploys to &lt;target&gt; last fail", "what's the probe history on win11-test", "audit the recent health checks", "did the demo target ever go red", or any "history" / "past" / "when did" intent against a target. ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-health-history))
- **signalman-inspect-release**: List or show the contents of signalman releases without rebuilding or redeploying them. Returns release rows, manifest sha256, signing fingerprint, and artifact metadata. Trigger when the user says "list releases", "show release &lt;id&gt;", "what releases of &lt;product&gt; do we have", "what's in release X", "find a release", "inspect the manifest", or any read-only "what did we build" intent. ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-inspect-release))
- **signalman-k8s-status**: Read deployment/release status in a Kubernetes namespace via `kubectl get deployments -o json` (default) or `helm status -o json`. Trigger on "what's running in namespace foo", "are the pods ready", "is my deploy healthy", "show me deployment state". Normalises across kubectl + helm into a single workload list with a derived state (healthy / degraded / unknown). ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-k8s-status))
- **signalman-key-management**: Generate a fresh Ed25519 signing keypair for Signalman release signing, or compute the fingerprint of an existing public key. Trigger when the user says "generate signing keys", "mint a release signing key", "what's the fingerprint of this key", "make me a new Ed25519 keypair", "key gen", or "check this key's fingerprint". ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-key-management))
- **signalman-list-cloud-instances**: Enumerate Signalman-tagged cloud instances for a given provider. Filters by signalman-managed=true internally — only Signalman-provisioned VMs surface, never operator-owned workloads. Trigger on "what cloud VMs are running", "list test boxes on AWS", "audit signalman cloud usage", "find that VM from yesterday". Use for audit, recovery, and cost-reaper sanity checks. v0.3.0-5: returned handles carry `tags` + `network_mode` (read by the reaper). CLI parity: `signalman cloud list`. ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-list-cloud-instances))
- **signalman-list-runners**: List registered Signalman build runners and their last-seen timestamps. Each row carries an `is_stale` flag computed from `last_seen_at` plus a configurable threshold. Trigger when the user says "list runners", "what runners are alive", "is the runner heartbeating", "show registered workers", "any builders online", or any "who's polling the queue" intent. ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-list-runners))
- **signalman-manage-cloud-budget**: Set or inspect a per-org monthly cloud-spend budget. Trigger when the user says "set a budget for org X", "what's our cloud spend this month", "limit AWS spend to $500/month", "why did this provision fail with budget_exceeded". Per-org budgets gate `signalman_cloud_provision` — over-budget calls refuse with `budget_exceeded` BEFORE any vendor API call. ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-manage-cloud-budget))
- **signalman-manage-cloud-credentials**: Set, view, or remove per-org cloud credentials stored encrypted at rest. Trigger when the user says "store AWS credentials for org X", "rotate the Azure service principal", "show me what credentials are configured", "remove the creds for the test org". The plaintext is encrypted with AES-256-GCM via `SIGNALMAN_CRED_KEY`; reads return ONLY a redacted hint (`AKIA****EXAMPLE`), never the secret. Provision automatically uses per-org credentials when `org_id` is passed. ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-manage-cloud-credentials))
- **signalman-mint-api-key**: Mint, list, or revoke a Signalman bearer-token API key. Trigger when the user says "mint an api key", "create a token for the runner", "give CI an api key", "list active api keys", "revoke key &lt;id&gt;", "rotate the runner credential", or any "bearer token" / "api key" intent against the control plane. ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-mint-api-key))
- **signalman-npm-bootstrap**: Set up the Signalman registry as an npm registry for the operator's org — publish packages to it, install from it, and transparently mirror npmjs.com through it. Per-org namespacing under /npm/&lt;org&gt;/ with scoped + unscoped package support. Virtual upstreams with optional Ed25519 re-signing give compliance-grade provenance for proxied npmjs.com packages. Trigger when the user says "publish my npm package to signalman", "set up the registry to mirror npmjs.com", "configure npm virtual registry", "bootstrap node package hosting", or asks how to use the registry as an npm backend. CLI parity: `si… ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-npm-bootstrap))
- **signalman-plan-scenario**: Dry-run a signalman scenario without executing it. Resolves parameter overrides, validates the loaded setup/assertions/workflow, and returns the step plan plus the resources the run would touch. Trigger when the user says "plan &lt;scenario&gt;", "dry-run &lt;scenario&gt;", "what would &lt;scenario&gt; do", "preview the steps for &lt;scenario&gt;", "validate the parameter overrides", or any variant of "check before I run." ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-plan-scenario))
- **signalman-promote-release**: Drive Signalman's auto-promotion + approval pipeline end-to-end. Configures promotion policies (auto / manual / time-delay); lists active policies; surfaces pending approvals; fires approve/reject decisions; and ticks the time-delay queue. Trigger when the user says "set up auto-promotion", "promote releases from test to demo", "approve the pending promotion", "what is pending approval", "show pending approvals", "list promotion policies", "tick the auto-approve queue", or asks about gate behaviour for a product. CLI parity: `signalman promotion {add,list,remove,approve,reject,approvals,tick}… ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-promote-release))
- **signalman-provision-cloud-vm**: Provision an ephemeral cloud VM (AWS EC2 or Azure VM) tagged with signalman-managed=true so the cost-reaper owns its TTL. Trigger when the user says "spin up an EC2 box", "give me an Azure VM for scenario X", "provision a cloud target", or any variant of "create a test VM on AWS / Azure". Returns a handle the operator can pass to status / terminate. v0.3.0-5: budget-gated (over-budget orgs refuse with `budget_exceeded`); `network.mode` supports public_mtls / aws_ssm / azure_bastion; per-org credentials inject when org_id is set and a cred row exists. CLI parity: `signalman cloud provision`. ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-provision-cloud-vm))
- **signalman-query-audit-log**: Query and append entries to the Signalman audit log. The log is immutable, append-only — no update or delete. Trigger on "what happened to deployment X", "show audit log for target Y", "who deployed v1.2.3", "record a manual restart in audit", "audit entries since yesterday". Use for forensic / postmortem / compliance reviews and for operators to log out-of-band gestures alongside the executor-driven appends. CLI parity: `signalman audit query|append`. ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-query-audit-log))
- **signalman-reap-cloud-instances**: Run the cost-reaper to terminate Signalman-managed cloud VMs whose TTL has expired. Trigger when the user says "clean up past-TTL VMs", "run the reaper", "kill anything expired in AWS/Azure", or asks why a forgotten test VM is still running. Each provisioned VM carries a `signalman-ttl-expires-at` tag (epoch seconds); the reaper polls every registered backend, terminates expired instances via the backend's idempotent terminate, and reports per-backend counts. ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-reap-cloud-instances))
- **signalman-record-scenario**: Capture an ad-hoc agent-driven investigation as a reusable signalman scenario by starting a recording session, doing work, then finalising into setup/workflow/assertions placeholders for operator review. Trigger when the user says "record this as a scenario", "save what we're doing", "turn this session into a scenario", "start a recording", "finalise the recording", "promote rec_&lt;id&gt;", or any variant of "make this repeatable". ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-record-scenario))
- **signalman-register-product**: Register, list, or soft-delete a signalman product (the external git repo whose tags signalman will build). Trigger when the user says "add product &lt;name&gt;", "register &lt;repo&gt;", "what products are registered", "list products", "remove product &lt;name&gt;", or hits a ProductNotFoundError from release build and needs to register first. ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-register-product))
- **signalman-register-runner**: Register a Signalman build runner against a control plane. Two-step flow: build + validate the config envelope, then persist it to disk. Trigger when the user says "register a runner", "set up a build worker", "wire this machine into the control plane", "save runner config", "configure runner pointing at &lt;URL&gt;", or any "this is my new build runner" intent. ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-register-runner))
- **signalman-register-target**: Register, list, or soft-delete a signalman deploy target (a VM or docker-compose host that release deploy can push onto). Trigger when the user says "add target &lt;name&gt;", "register a test VM", "register a docker target", "list targets", "remove target &lt;name&gt;", or hits a TargetNotFoundError from release deploy and needs to register first. ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-register-target))
- **signalman-rollback**: Roll back a signalman target by redeploying its previous-active release. Triggers on "roll back &lt;target&gt;", "undo the last deploy", "revert &lt;target&gt; to the previous version". For an explicit older release, takes `--to-release`. ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-rollback))
- **signalman-rollback-k8s**: Roll back a Kubernetes release via `kubectl rollout undo` or `helm rollback`. Trigger on "rollback the k8s deploy", "undo the helm release", "revert deployment/foo in namespace bar". For kubectl, the release id is the rollout subject (e.g. `deployment/my-app`); for helm, it's the Helm release name. Optional `to_revision` pins an explicit revision; omitting picks the immediately-preceding one. ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-rollback-k8s))
- **signalman-run-scenario**: Execute a signalman scenario and stream its events. Returns a run handle synchronously; events drain incrementally via signalman_status long-poll until the run terminates. Trigger when the user says "run &lt;scenario&gt;", "execute &lt;scenario&gt;", "kick off &lt;scenario&gt;", "do &lt;scenario&gt; on &lt;target&gt;", "test &lt;product&gt; with scenario X", or any clear "run this now" intent. ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-run-scenario))
- **signalman-schedule-health**: Set up periodic health-check schedules against deployed targets and manually trigger one-shot tick runs. Lists existing schedules, registers a new one with an interval and (optional) probe-name filter, surfaces disable/enable/remove, and fires `signalman_schedule_run_once` for verify-before-daemon flows or CI cron paths. Trigger when the user says "schedule health checks for &lt;target&gt;", "ping &lt;target&gt; every N minutes", "set up periodic probes on &lt;target&gt;", "run the scheduler once", "fire all schedules now", "verify the schedule fires", or asks to list/disable an existing schedule. CLI parity:… ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-schedule-health))
- **signalman-terminate-cloud-vm**: Terminate an ephemeral cloud VM previously provisioned by Signalman. Idempotent — terminating an already-terminated handle returns success, not an error. Trigger on "kill that EC2 box", "tear down the Azure VM", "destroy &lt;handle&gt;", or any "clean up the test VM" variant. Refuses untagged instances (only Signalman-managed handles can be terminated through this path). For bulk past-TTL cleanup, use `signalman-reap-cloud-instances` instead. CLI parity: `signalman cloud terminate`. ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-terminate-cloud-vm))
- **signalman-verify-release**: Verify a release's Ed25519 manifest signature against a public key. Confirms both the fingerprint match (the public key signs releases with this `signed_by` value) and the cryptographic signature over the canonical manifest. Trigger when the user says "verify release &lt;id&gt;", "check the signature on &lt;release&gt;", "is &lt;release&gt; trustworthy", "confirm this build was signed by &lt;key&gt;", or any "is this what I think it is" intent against a release. ([ambray/signalman](https://github.com/ambray/signalman/tree/HEAD/skills/signalman-verify-release))
- **signalmc-med-eval**: This benchmark evaluates biosignal foundation models on synchronized, long-duration single-lead ECG and PPG recordings from emergency department visits. It probes the models' ability to extract clinically meaningful representations for tasks such as age and sex prediction, emergency disposition, laboratory value regression, and ICD-10 diagnosis classification. Use when the user wants to benchmark on SignalMC-MED, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports AUROC. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/signalmc-med-eval) · ★3)
- **signalmode**: Signal over noise. Forces the AI to drop filler, keep substance, and respond with precision. Cuts token waste, reduces cost, and makes every response actually useful. LLM-agnostic. Works across all major AI coding agents. ([wfprieto/SignalMode](https://github.com/wfprieto/SignalMode/tree/HEAD/skills/signalmode))
- **signalmode-basic-english**: Forces plain, layman-friendly output reports. Stoic, pragmatic, and simple. Assumes the reader knows nothing about code, cloud platforms, databases, or technical services. Every response follows a strict 5-section structure. Short, punchy, easy to read. No jargon without explanation. ([wfprieto/SignalMode](https://github.com/wfprieto/SignalMode/tree/HEAD/skills/signalmode-basic-english))
- **signalmode-commit**: Write precise, conventional commit messages. Reads the staged diff and produces a commit message that is accurate, complete, and follows Conventional Commits format. No filler. No vague summaries. ([wfprieto/SignalMode](https://github.com/wfprieto/SignalMode/tree/HEAD/skills/signalmode-commit))
- **signalmode-compress**: Compress natural language files (CLAUDE.md, AGENTS.md, todos, preferences, project notes) into SignalMode-speak to reduce input tokens on every future session. Compressed version overwrites the original. Human-readable backup saved automatically as FILE.original.md. ([wfprieto/SignalMode](https://github.com/wfprieto/SignalMode/tree/HEAD/skills/signalmode-compress))
- **signalmode-help**: Show available SignalMode skills, commands, and usage. Triggers on /signalmode-help or when user asks what SignalMode can do. ([wfprieto/SignalMode](https://github.com/wfprieto/SignalMode/tree/HEAD/skills/signalmode-help))
- **signalmode-human**: Protected anti-AI writing hygiene rules. Replaces habitual AI language with clean prose. ([wfprieto/SignalMode](https://github.com/wfprieto/SignalMode/tree/HEAD/skills/signalmode-human))
- **signalmode-refresh**: Compiles the active session into a lean, verifiable continuation contract. Use when a session is becoming long, outputs are drifting, work must move to a new session, or a project requires a portable handoff to a human or another agent. Preserves the active objective, confirmed state, decisions, constraints, risks, recovery path, and next executable actions while excluding transcript noise and inaccessible context. ([wfprieto/SignalMode](https://github.com/wfprieto/SignalMode/tree/HEAD/skills/signalmode-refresh))
- **signalmode-review**: Adversarial code review pass. Junior output in, senior output out. Reviews code, PRs, diffs, or architecture decisions with the lens of a senior engineer who has seen every failure mode. No flattery. No hedging. Only what matters. ([wfprieto/SignalMode](https://github.com/wfprieto/SignalMode/tree/HEAD/skills/signalmode-review))
- **signalmode-stats**: Show real token usage and estimated savings for the current session. Reads directly from the agent session log — no AI estimation. Triggers on /signalmode-stats. Output is injected by the mode-tracker hook; the model itself does not compute the numbers. ([wfprieto/SignalMode](https://github.com/wfprieto/SignalMode/tree/HEAD/skills/signalmode-stats))
- **signalmode-voice**: A personal writing voice template. Defines your preferred output structure, tone, and judgment standard. Fully customizable by the user. The defaults ship as The Strategist voice: verdict-first, calm authority, no corporate theater. Edit this file to make it your own. ([wfprieto/SignalMode](https://github.com/wfprieto/SignalMode/tree/HEAD/skills/signalmode-voice))
- **signalnoiseratio**: Compute the SignalNoiseRatio metric — provided by torchmetrics. Use when the user has predictions and ground-truth and needs to compute SignalNoiseRatio, or asks how to score with SignalNoiseRatio. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/signalnoiseratio) · ★3)
- **signalp-run**: Run SignalP 6.0 (via Docker) on genome protein FASTA files to predict signal peptides (SP, LIPO, TAT, PILIN). Reads genomes from cyanobacteria_genomes.csv, skips already-predicted strains unless --force is given. Results are written to cache/&lt;organism&gt;/genomes/&lt;strain&gt;/signalp/.
